Sub DoEvents_check_exit()
    Do
        Call VB40032.rtcDoEvents()
    Loop While exit_flag = 1
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub timer_entry_stub()  'MethCallEngine entry
    If exit_flag = 1 Then
        Call stop_app_and_music()
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub battle_set_action_code()
    battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).actionType = 5
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub swap_values(ByRef v1, ByRef v2)
    retval = v1
    v1 = v2
    v2 = retval
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub calc_sprite_y_offset(ByRef enemyIdx, ByRef multiplier)
    retval = (enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).level + 6) * multiplier
    Exit Sub
End Sub

    Exit Sub

Sub check_key_pressed()
    Call DoEvents_check_exit()
    result = PAL_CheckKey(key_state_array(0))
    retval = result
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub increment_script_ip(ByRef ip)
    If ip = 32767 Then
        retval = -32768
        Exit Sub
    End If
    retval = ip + 1
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub read_key()
    key_pressed = 0
    While key_pressed = 0
        key_pressed = check_key_pressed()
    Wend
    retval = key_pressed
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub play_sound(ByRef soundNum, ByRef channel)
    If has_sfx_raw <> 0 Then
        If has_sfx And (soundNum > 0) Then
            Call PAL_PlayDSound(soundNum, channel)
        End If
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

    Next

End Sub

    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub wait_frame(ByRef frames)
    For i = 1 To frames
        result = check_key_pressed()
        If result Then
            Exit Sub
        End If
        Call PAL_WaitTime(1)
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

    Exit Sub
End Sub

    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub check_battle_action(ByRef actionParam, ByRef directionParam)
    If battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).actionType = 8 Then
        battle_curr_role_idx = enemy_pos_count
    End If
    battle_curr_role_idx = battle_curr_role_idx + 1
    actionParam = 0
    directionParam = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub push_screen_buffer()
    If screen_buffer_ptr = 0 Then
        Call PAL_PushScreen(screen_buf(0))
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Call PAL_CopyMem(screen_buf(0), screen_buffer_ptr, 64000)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub check_trigger_flag()
    If flag_key_updown Then
        Call wait_frame(160)
        If flag_parallel_mutex Then
            Call restore_screen()
        End If
        GoTo L_004038E8
    End If
    If flag_trigger Then
        Call show_dialog_image_and_wait()
    End If
L_004038E8:
    flag_trigger = 0
    flag_key_updown = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub random_enemy_id()

    While enemy_battle_data(rng).hp <= 0
        rng = Int (VB40032.rtcRandomNext() * CSng (enemy_max_id))
    Wend
    retval = rng
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub read_file_and_close(filename, subfileNum)
    result = open_file(filename, 0)
    tmp_file_size = get_subfile_len(result, subfileNum)
    Call kernel32._hread(result, global_buf_2(0), tmp_file_size)
    Call kernel32._lclose(result)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub load_fbp_subfile(subfileNum)

    Exit Sub
End Sub

    x = npc_curr_frame - (mgo_frame_offsets(retval) \ 2)

    Exit Sub
End Sub

    If result >= 0 Then
        j = inventory(result).amount
    Else

    End If
    j = j + count_equipped_items(itemID)
    retval = j
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub restore_screen()
    If screen_buffer_ptr = 0 Then
        Call PAL_PopScreen(screen_buf(0))
    Else
        Call PAL_CopyMem(screen_buffer_ptr, screen_buf(0), 64000)
    End If
    mutex_can_change_palette = 0
    flag_parallel_mutex = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

    If tmp2 = 0 Then

    End If
    If tmp2 = 1 Then
        Call select_magic()
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

End Sub

End Sub

Sub find_inventory_item(ByRef itemID)
    foundIdx = -1
    For i = 0 To 255
        If (inventory(i).amount > 0) And (inventory(i).item = itemID) Then
            foundIdx = i
        End If
    Next
    retval = foundIdx
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub update_shake()
    If shake_active Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If shake_intensity > 0 Then
        shake_intensity = shake_intensity - 1
        If shake_intensity < 16 Then
            shake_duration = (shake_duration * 15) \ 16
        End If
        retval = (shake_intensity And 1) * shake_duration
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

    auto_battle_flag = 1
    battle_role_idx_2 = 0
    retval = result
    Exit Sub
End Sub

    Exit Sub
End Sub

End Sub

Sub check_in_battle(shakeMode)
    mutex_shaking = mutex_shaking Xor 1
    Call PAL_ClearTree()
    If flag_battling Then
        Call draw_battle_scene(1, 0)
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(global_buf_2(0), 8192)
    Call get_sprites_curr_scene()
    Call get_party_role_id()
    Call render_scene_with_rng()
    Call render_scene_and_fade(shakeMode)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub menu_Status()
    retval = 0
    While (retval >= 0) And (retval <= RPG_team_number)
        Call show_role_status(retval)
        If (key_pressed = 3) Or (key_pressed = 5) Then
            retval = retval - 1
        Else
            retval = retval + 1
        End If
        If key_pressed = 1 Then
            retval = -1
        End If
    Wend
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub update_party_position()
    party_abs_x = team_abstract_x + RPG_viewport_x
    party_abs_y = team_abstract_y + RPG_viewport_y
    If (party_abs_x <> x_off) Or (party_abs_y <> y_off) Then
        Call update_walk_frame()
    Else
        viewport_flags_2 = 0
        viewport_flags = 0
        scene_flags = (scene_flags And 2) Xor 2
    End If
    Call set_team_draw()
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub update_walk_frame()
    scene_flags = (scene_flags + 1) And 3
    If (scene_flags And 1) = 0 Then
        viewport_flags = 0
        viewport_flags_2 = viewport_flags
    Else
        viewport_flags = (scene_flags + 1) \ 2
        viewport_flags_2 = 3 - viewport_flags
    End If
    Call PAL_Rhrff(trail(0).x)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub calc_magic_damage(ByRef baseDamage, ByRef elementIdx)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(save_temp_buf(0), data_battlefield(12 * RPG_battle_scene_number), 12)
    i = baseDamage
    If (elementIdx > 0) And (elementIdx <= 5) Then
        i = CSng (baseDamage * (10 + save_temp_buf(elementIdx))) / CSng (10)
    End If
    retval = i
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub read_file_to_buf(subfileNum, ByRef destIdx)

    Call PAL_Unpak(global_buf_2(0), mgo_frame_offsets(destIdx))
    retval = PAL_PakSize(global_buf_2(0))
    npc_frame_base = retval
    Exit Sub
End Sub

        rng = Int (VB40032.rtcRandomNext() * CSng (battle_extra_param))

Sub reset_battle_sprite_pos(ByRef roleIdx)
    player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).x = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).origX
    player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).y = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).origY
    player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).direction = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).origDirection
    player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).animOffset = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub read_mkf_subfile(ByRef subfileNum, ByRef destBuf)
    filePos = kernel32._llseek(fh_temp, file_offset_table(subfileNum), 0)
    tmp = filePos
    tmp_file_size = file_offset_table(subfileNum + 1) - file_offset_table(subfileNum)
    AryLock mem=seekResult
    Call kernel32._hread(fh_temp, destBuf(0), tmp_file_size)
    AryUnlock mem=seekResult
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub load_battle_sprites()
    enemy_pos_count = RPG_team_number
    battle_extra_param = enemy_pos_count + 1
    retval = 0
    For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
        player_battle_sprite(i).spriteBase = retval
        Call read_file_to_buf(player_battle_sprite(i).spriteNum, retval)
        retval = retval + npc_frame_base
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub read_rng_subfile(subfileNum)
    tmp_file_size = get_subfile_len(fh_RNG_MKF, subfileNum)
    If tmp_file_size > max_subfile_size Then
        Redim dims=1 desc=00000000 flags=0001,0000
        max_subfile_size = tmp_file_size
    End If
    AryLock mem=lockMem
    Call kernel32._hread(fh_RNG_MKF, rng_anim_data(0), tmp_file_size)
    AryUnlock mem=lockMem
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub open_file(ByRef filename, ByRef writeMode)
    If writeMode Then
        fh = kernel32._lcreat(filename, 0)
        tmp2 = fh
    Else
        fh = kernel32._lopen(filename, 0)
        tmp2 = fh
    End If
    tmp = tmp2
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub load_sound_data()
    Call PAL_LoadDSound(27, 1)
    Call PAL_LoadDSound(28, 1)
    Call PAL_LoadDSound(29, 1)
    Call PAL_LoadDSound(30, 1)
    Call PAL_LoadDSound(45, 1)
    Call PAL_LoadDSound(212, 1)
    Call PAL_LoadDSound(47, 1)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub load_theurgy_image(ByRef magicIdx)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(tmp, data_magic(magicIdx * 32), 2)
    If tmp < 55 Then
        filename = 'FIRE.MKF'
        Call read_file_and_close(filename, tmp)
        Call PAL_Unpak(global_buf_2(0), fire_mkf_data(0))
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub count_equipped_items(ByRef itemID)
    count = 0
    For i = 0 To RPG_team_number
        k = party(i).role
        For j = 11 To 16
            If playerRoles(k, j) = itemID Then
                count = count + 1
            End If
        Next
    Next
    retval = count
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub calc_battle_damage(ByRef attack, ByRef defense)
    i = 0
    If attack > 0 Then
        j = attack - defense
        k = (CSng (defense) / CSng (5)) * CSng (3)
        If attack > k Then
            i = attack - k
            If j > 0 Then
                i = i + j
            End If
        End If
        i = i \ 2
        If i < 0 Then
            i = 0
        End If
    End If
    damage = i
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub get_subfile_len(ByRef fileHandle, ByRef subfileNum)
    seekResult = kernel32._llseek(fileHandle, subfileNum * 4, 0)
    tmp = seekResult
    Call kernel32._hread(fileHandle, file_offset_table(0), 8)
    seekResult = kernel32._llseek(fileHandle, file_offset_table(0), 0)
    tmp = seekResult
    fileSize = file_offset_table(1) - file_offset_table(0)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub frame_menu(x, y, ByRef rowCount, colCount)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(global_buf_2(0), menu_bg_data(0), menu_bg_size)
    save_temp_buf(0) = 44
    For i = 1 To rowCount
        save_temp_buf(i) = 45
    Next
    save_temp_buf(i) = 46
    Call draw_menu_frame(x, y, rowCount + 1, colCount)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub load_scene_events(ByRef sceneID)
    curr_scene_event_count = scenes(sceneID + 1).eventObjectIndex - scenes(sceneID).eventObjectIndex
    If curr_scene_event_count > 160 Then
        curr_scene_event_count = 160
    End If
    Call PAL_CopyMem(npc_display_data(1).vanishTime, events(scenes(sceneID).eventObjectIndex * 32), curr_scene_event_count * 32)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub draw_battle_status_bar()
    dialog_y_pos = 200
    Call draw_battle_scene(1, -4)
    dialog_y_pos = 140
    For i = 0 To 3
        Call PAL_PutP(key_scan_map(i), key_action_map(i), word_glyph_index(word_glyph_index(40 + i)), screen_buffer_ptr, 4, 2)
    Next
    Call show_status_icons()
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub fade_out_palette(ByRef speed)
    If palette_fade_active Then
        palette_fade_active = 0
        If speed > 0 Then
            For i = 6 To 63
                Call PAL_ExPalate(palette_data(768), palette_data(RPG_color_begin_ptr), 768, i)
                Call PAL_IntPalate(palette_data(768))
                Call PAL_WaitTime(speed)
            Next
        End If
        Call PAL_IntPalate(palette_data(RPG_color_begin_ptr))
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub draw_menu_with_text_and_hp(ByRef wordIdx, ByRef x, ByRef y, hp, labelIdx)
    Call frame_menu(x, y, 5, labelIdx)
    Call draw_string(x + 10, y + 10, word_dat_data(wordIdx), 3, 0)
    Call show_small_number(x + 48, y + 15, hp, 6)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub release_resources_exit()
    Call kernel32._lclose(fh_ABC_MKF)
    Call kernel32._lclose(fh_F_MKF)
    Call kernel32._lclose(fh_RNG_MKF)
    Call kernel32._lclose(fh_MGO_MKF)
    Close 1
    Erase rng_anim_data
    Call PAL_ShutdownDSound()
    Call cd_stopplay()
    Call midi_close()
    Call PAL_KillTimer()
    Call PAL_ShutDownInput()
    Call PAL_ResetMode()
    Call Me.method_002C(vb_com_object_1.f0000, vb_com_object_2.f0000)
    End
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub trim_string(ByRef str)
    strLen = Len (str)
    Do
        substr = Mid(str, 1, strLen)
        charCode = VB40032.rtcAnsiValueBstr(Mid(str, 1, strLen))
        strLen = strLen - 1
    Loop While (charCode = 0) Or (charCode = 32)
    result = Left(str, strLen + 1)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub make_dialog_frame(ByRef x, ByRef y)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(global_buf_2(0), menu_bg_data(0), menu_bg_size)
    Call PAL_PutP(x + 3, y + 2, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(69)), screen_buffer_ptr, 0, 3)
    Call PAL_PutP(x, y, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(69)), screen_buffer_ptr, 0, 0)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub unequip_item(ByRef itemID, ByRef count)
    For i = 1 To count
        For j = 0 To RPG_team_number
            l = party(j).role
            For k = 11 To 16
                If playerRoles(l, k) = itemID Then
                    playerRoles(l, k) = 0
                    GoTo L_0040595E
                End If
            Next
        Next
L_0040595E:
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub fade_palette_to(ByRef speed)
    Call PAL_FuPalate(speed, palette_data(1152), palette_data(RPG_color_begin_ptr))
    Call PAL_CopyMem(palette_data(768), palette_data(RPG_color_begin_ptr), 768)
    For i = 1 To 25
        Call PAL_CvPalate(palette_data(768), palette_data(1152))
        Call PAL_IntPalate(palette_data(768))
        Call PAL_WaitTime(8)
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub show_face(ByRef x, ByRef y, faceID)
    filename = 'RGM.MKF'
    Call read_file_and_close(filename, faceID)
    faceIdx = global_buf_2(0)
    x = x - (global_buf_2(faceIdx) \ 2)
    y = y - (global_buf_2(faceIdx + 1) \ 2)
    Call PAL_PutP(x, y, global_buf_2(faceIdx), screen_buffer_ptr, 0, 0)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub process_menu()
    Call draw_menu_with_text_and_hp(21, 0, 0, RPG_money, -1)
    retval = menu_loop(tmp, 3, 37, 3, 3, 4)
    tmp2 = retval
    Select Case tmp2
        Case 0
            Call entry_stub_show_text()
        Case 1
            Call use_item_menu()
        Case 2
            Call menu_inventory()
        Case 3
            Call menu_system()
    End Select
    Call draw_battle_row_sprite()
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub draw_battle_row_sprite()
    Call PAL_ExMyll(viewport_width)
    Call PAL_VWindow(0, 0, 319, 199)
    relative_viewport_x = 0
    relative_viewport_y = 0
    viewport_x_bak = RPG_viewport_x
    viewport_y_bak = RPG_viewport_y
    j = RPG_viewport_x
    k = RPG_viewport_y
    Call PAL_ExRij(i, j, k)
    Call PAL_VMap(j, k, i, map_data_buf(0), mgo_frame_offsets(0), global_buf_1(0))
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub enemy_cast_anim(ByRef enemyIdx)
    retval = (enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).idleFrames + enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).magicFrames) - 1
    If enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).magicFrames > 0 Then
        For i = enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).idleFrames To retval
            enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).direction = i
            Call draw_battle_scene(enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).actWaitFrames, 0)
        Next
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Call draw_battle_scene(1, 0)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub show_fbp_picture(ByRef subfileNum, ByRef rngSubfile)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(bg_buf(0), global_buf_1(0), 64000)
    If subfileNum >= 0 Then
        Call load_fbp_subfile(subfileNum)
    Else
        Call PAL_ClearMenu(global_buf_1(0), 32000)
    End If
    If rngSubfile Then
        Call draw_rng_frame(rngSubfile, 95, 10672)
    Else
        Call PAL_PopScreen(global_buf_1(0))
    End If
    Call PAL_CopyMem(global_buf_1(0), bg_buf(0), 64000)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub show_number_to_tree(ByRef x, ByRef y, ByRef number, ByRef colorType)
    If number < 0 Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If colorType = 0 Then
        colorBase = 19
    End If
    If colorType = 1 Then
        colorBase = 29
    End If
    If colorType = 2 Then
        colorBase = 56
    End If
    screenX = x
    remaining = number
    While remaining > 0
        digit = remaining Mod 10
        remaining = remaining \ 10
        Call PAL_AddToTree(screenX, y + 999, 999, word_glyph_index(word_glyph_index(digit + colorBase)))
        screenX = screenX - 6
    Wend
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub cd_stop()
    If (music_mode = 2) And (cd_active <> 0) Then
        cd_track_valid = 0
        cd_active = 0
        cd_track_num = 0
        tmp4 = tmp
        mciResult = kernel32.mciSendStringA('stop cdtrack', tmp, 30, 0)
        tmp2 = mciResult
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub adjust_battle_sprite_pos()
    For i = 1 To 9
        For j = 0 To enemy_pos_count
            If check_player_alive(j) > 1 Then
                player_battle_sprite(j).x = (player_battle_sprite(j).x + battle_sprite_data(j)) \ 2
                player_battle_sprite(j).y = (player_battle_sprite(j).y + battle_sprite_data_ext(j)) \ 2
            End If
        Next
        Call draw_battle_scene(1, 0)
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub cross_fade_out(ByRef speed)
    palette_fade_active = 0
    retval = 0
    j = 64
    If speed = 0 Then
        speed = 1
    End If
    If speed < 0 Then
        Call swap_values(retval, j)
    End If
    For i = retval To j Step speed
        Call PAL_ExPalate(palette_data(768), palette_data(RPG_color_begin_ptr), 768, i)
        Call PAL_IntPalate(palette_data(768))
        Call process_event_objects(0)
        Call check_in_battle(0)
    Next
    palette_fade_active = speed < 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub load_script_data(ByRef subfileNum)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(file_offset_table(0), sss_script_data(subfileNum * 4), 8)
    fileSize = file_offset_table(1) - file_offset_table(0)
    image_offset_table(0) = fileSize
    seekResult = kernel32._llseek(fh_M_MSG, file_offset_table(0), 0)
    tmp = seekResult
    Call kernel32._hread(fh_M_MSG, image_offset_table(1), fileSize)
    image_offset_table(fileSize + 1) = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub sell_item_menu()
    menuItemSelected = 0
    result = 0
    Do
        Call draw_menu_with_text_and_hp(21, 100, 150, RPG_money, 0)
        retval = select_item_with_filter(menuItemSelected, 1, 32)
        If retval >= 0 Then
            Call push_screen_buffer()
            tmp = yes_no_menu(result, 19)
            If tmp = 1 Then
                Call remove_inventory_item(retval, 1)
                RPG_money = RPG_money + (objects(retval).data1 \ 2)
            End If
            Call restore_screen()
        End If
    Loop While retval >= 0
    Call draw_battle_row_sprite()
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub get_sprite_frame_data(ByRef roleIdx, ByRef frame)
    retval = mgo_frame_offsets(player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).spriteBase + player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).direction) + player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).spriteBase
    baseIdx = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).x - (mgo_frame_offsets(retval) \ 2)
    y = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).y - mgo_frame_offsets(retval + 1)
    Call PAL_PutP(baseIdx, y, mgo_frame_offsets(retval), screen_buffer_ptr, frame, 1)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub fade_in(ByRef speed)
    If palette_fade_active = 0 Then
        palette_fade_active = 1
        If speed > 0 Then
            For i = 63 To 5 Step -1
                Call PAL_ExPalate(palette_data(768), palette_data(RPG_color_begin_ptr), 768, i)
                Call PAL_IntPalate(palette_data(768))
                Call PAL_WaitTime(speed)
            Next
        End If
        Call PAL_ExPalate(palette_data(768), palette_data(RPG_color_begin_ptr), 768, 0)
        Call PAL_IntPalate(palette_data(768))
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub update_damage_numbers()
    For i = 0 To 11
        If damage_numbers(i).timer > 0 Then
            Call show_number_to_tree(damage_numbers(i).colorType, damage_numbers(i).number, damage_numbers(i).y, damage_numbers(i).x)
            damage_numbers(i).timer = damage_numbers(i).timer - 1
            damage_numbers(i).number = damage_numbers(i).number - 1
        End If
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub play_battle_effect(ByRef roleIdx)
    Call load_battle_effect_sprites()
    effect_x_coords(1) = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).x
    effect_y_coords(1) = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).y
    effect_frames(1) = 100
    effect_particle_count = 1
    effect_frame_count = 14 + (battle_effect_data(player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).spriteNum).effect * 10)
    For i = 1 To 10
        effect_frame_count = effect_frame_count + 1
        Call draw_battle_scene(1, -1)
    Next
    effect_particle_count = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub show_enemy_damage(ByRef thyrgyType)
    For i = 0 To team_number
        If battle_enemy_data_ext(i) > 0 Then
            j = enemy_battle_data(i).hp - battle_enemy_data_ext(i)
            If j <> 0 Then
                If j > 0 Then
                    k = 0
                Else
                    k = 1
                End If
                Call add_damage_number(enemy_battle_data(i).x, enemy_battle_data(i).y - 110, Abs (j), k)
                thyrgyType = -1
                player_hit_flags(i) = -1
            End If
        End If
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub draw_menu_frame(ByRef x, ByRef y, ByRef rowCount, ByRef highlight)
    retval = x
    For i = 0 To rowCount
        k = global_buf_2(save_temp_buf(i))
        If highlight Or ((i = rowCount) And highlight) Then
            Call PAL_PutP(retval + 6, y + 6, global_buf_2(k), screen_buffer_ptr, 0, 3)
        End If
        Call PAL_PutP(retval, y, global_buf_2(k), screen_buffer_ptr, 0, 0)
        retval = retval + global_buf_2(k)
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub add_damage_number(ByRef colorType, ByRef number, ByRef y, ByRef x)
    If y <= 0 Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    For i = 0 To 11
        If damage_numbers(i).timer <= 0 Then
            damage_numbers(i).timer = 10
            damage_numbers(i).colorType = colorType
            damage_numbers(i).number = number
            damage_numbers(i).y = y
            damage_numbers(i).x = x
            If damage_numbers(i).number < 15 Then
                damage_numbers(i).number = 15
            End If
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub apply_enemy_poison_damage()
    For i = 0 To team_number
        If battle_order_array(i) Then
            enemy_battle_data(i).hp = enemy_battle_data(i).hp - battle_order_array(20 + i)
            If enemy_battle_data(i).hp > 0 Then
                enemy_battle_data(i).x = enemy_battle_data(i).origX
                enemy_battle_data(i).y = enemy_battle_data(i).origY
            Else
                battle_enemy_hp = enemy_runtime_data(i).deathSound
            End If
        End If
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub check_trigger_events()
    retval = produce_screen_map(party_abs_x, party_abs_y, RPG_team_direction)
    If retval >= 0 Then
        If npc_display_data(retval).currentFrame < (npc_display_data(retval).spriteFrames * 4) Then
            npc_display_data(retval).currentFrame = 0
            npc_display_data(retval).direction = (RPG_team_direction + 2) And 3
            For i = 0 To RPG_team_number
                party(i).frame = RPG_team_direction * 3
            Next
            Call check_in_battle(0)
        End If
        Call process_Script(retval, npc_display_data(retval).triggerScript)
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub fade_in_or_out_internal()  'MethCallEngine entry
    Call Me.method_0064(Me.method_0010(Me.method_006C(Me.method_0010(Me.method_0054(Me.method_005C(CSng (0), vb_com_object_1.f0000), CSng (0), vb_com_object_1.f0000), j, vb_com_object_2.f0000), j, tmp, vb_com_object_1.f0000), tmp2, vb_com_object_2.f0000), tmp2, tmp, vb_com_object_1.f0000)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub check_player_alive(ByRef roleIdx)
    i = -1
    i = i And (player_status(roleIdx, 0) <= 0)
    i = i And (player_status(roleIdx, 1) <= 0)
    i = i And (player_status(roleIdx, 2) <= 0)
    i = i And (playerRoles(party(roleIdx).role, 9) > 0)
    If i Then
        If player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).direction = 1 Then
            i = 1
        Else
            i = 2
        End If
    End If
    retval = i
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub menu_select_party(x, y, ByRef spriteNum)
    i = 0
    If RPG_team_number > 0 Then
        If j > RPG_team_number Then
            j = RPG_team_number
        End If
        If RPG_team_number > 0 Then
            For k = 0 To RPG_team_number
                battle_order_array(k) = playerRoles(party(k).role, 3)
                Call PAL_GetBin(battle_order_array(100 + k), spriteNum, party(k).role)
            Next
            i = menu_select(j, x, y, 3, RPG_team_number + 1)
        End If
    End If
    retval = i
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub select_magic()
L_00407140:
    retval = select_item_with_filter(magic_select_tmp, 0, 1)
    If retval < 0 Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Call PAL_GetBin(magicCount, objects(retval).data6, 4)
    If magicCount = 0 Then
        Call show_item_description(i, 110, 2, magic_select_tmp)
        If i < 0 Then
            GoTo L_00407140
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Call draw_battle_row_sprite()
    j = 0
    Call process_Script(j, objects(retval).data2)
    If redraw_hp_mp_flag Then
        Call PAL_GetBin(magicCount, objects(retval).data6, 3)
        If magicCount Then
            Call remove_inventory_item(retval, 1)
        End If
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub draw_object_icon(ByRef x, ByRef y, ByRef invIdx)
    retval = objects(invIdx).data0
    If retval Then
        Call PAL_PutP(x + 5, y + 4, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(70)), screen_buffer_ptr, 0, 3)
        Call PAL_PutP(x, y, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(70)), screen_buffer_ptr, 0, 0)
        Call read_ball_mkf_index(retval)
        Call PAL_PutP(x + 8, y + 7, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(0)), screen_buffer_ptr, 0, 0)
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub show_money(ByRef amount)
    Call frame_menu(60, 100, 10, -1)
    Call draw_string(70, 110, word_dat_data(9), 3, 0)
    Call show_small_number(148, 114, amount, 6)
    Call draw_string(194, 110, word_dat_data(10), 3, 0)
    RPG_money = RPG_money + amount
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub init_enemy_positions()
    For i = 0 To team_number
        enemy_battle_data(i).x = enemy_pos_data(team_number, i).x
        enemy_battle_data(i).y = enemy_pos_data(team_number, i).y + enemy_runtime_data(i).yPosOffset
        enemy_battle_data(i).origX = enemy_battle_data(i).x
        enemy_battle_data(i).origY = enemy_battle_data(i).y
        enemy_battle_data(i).direction = 0
        enemy_battle_data(i).flag = 0
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub display_number(ByRef x, ByRef y, ByRef number, ByRef colorType)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(global_buf_2(0), menu_bg_data(0), menu_bg_size)
    If number < 0 Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If colorType = 0 Then
        colorBase = 19
    End If
    If colorType = 1 Then
        colorBase = 29
    End If
    If colorType = 2 Then
        colorBase = 56
    End If
    screenX = x
    remaining = number
    Do
        digit = remaining Mod 10
        remaining = remaining \ 10
        Call PAL_PutP(screenX, y, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(digit + colorBase)), screen_buffer_ptr, 0, 0)
        screenX = screenX - 6
    Loop While remaining > 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub show_small_number(ByRef x, ByRef y, ByRef number, ByRef digitCount)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(global_buf_2(0), menu_bg_data(0), menu_bg_size)
    If number < 0 Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    screenX = (x + (digitCount * 6)) - 6
    number = number
    For remaining = 1 To digitCount
        digit = number Mod 10
        number = Int (number / CSng (10))
        Call PAL_PutP(screenX, y, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(digit + 19)), screen_buffer_ptr, 0, 0)
        screenX = screenX - 6
        If number <= 0 Then
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub select_battle_target()
    If enemy_pos_count <= 0 Then
        i = 0
    Else
        battle_target_cursor = 0
        i = -2
        While i = -2
            result = check_key_pressed()
            If result = 5 Then
                battle_target_cursor = battle_target_cursor - 1
            End If
            If result = 6 Then
                battle_target_cursor = battle_target_cursor + 1
            End If
            If battle_target_cursor < 0 Then
                battle_target_cursor = enemy_pos_count
            End If
            If battle_target_cursor > enemy_pos_count Then
                battle_target_cursor = 0
            End If
            If result = 1 Then
                i = -1
            End If
            If result = 2 Then
                i = battle_target_cursor
            End If
            Call draw_battle_scene(1, 0)
        Wend
        battle_target_cursor = -1
    End If
    retval = i
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub add_inventory_item(ByRef itemID, ByRef amount)
    If itemID <= 0 Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    For i = 0 To 255
        If (inventory(i).amount > 0) And (inventory(i).item = itemID) Then
            inventory(i).amount = inventory(i).amount + amount
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next
    For i = 0 To 255
        If inventory(i).amount <= 0 Then
            inventory(i).amount = amount
            inventory(i).item = itemID
            inventory(i).amountInUse = 0
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub enemy_hit_reaction(ByRef shakeCount)
    retval = 8
    For i = 1 To shakeCount
        For j = 0 To 1
            For k = 0 To team_number
                If battle_order_array(k) Then
                    enemy_battle_data(k).x = enemy_battle_data(k).x - retval
                    enemy_battle_data(k).y = enemy_battle_data(k).y - (retval \ 2)
                    If i = 1 Then
                        player_hit_flags(k) = -1
                    End If
                End If
            Next
            Call draw_battle_scene(1, 0)
            retval = -retval
        Next
        retval = retval \ 2
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub play_rng_effect(rngSubfile, ByRef frameCount)
    retval = frameCount
    If retval = 0 Then
        retval = 88
    End If
    If flag_battling Then
        Call draw_enemy_battle_frame()
        j = 9060
    Else
        bufPtr = PAL_ArrayPtr(global_buf_1(64000))
        Call PAL_NipWSeg(bufPtr)
        Call draw_battle_row_sprite()
        Call PAL_RripAFreeze()
        Call check_in_battle(0)
        Call PAL_NipWSeg(screen_buffer_ptr)
        Call PAL_CopyMem(global_buf_1(0), global_buf_1(64000), 64000)
        j = 10668
    End If
    Call draw_rng_frame(rngSubfile, retval, j)
    If flag_battling Then
        Call PAL_CopyMem(global_buf_1(0), bg_buf(0), 64000)
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Call draw_battle_row_sprite()
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub draw_rng_frame(ByRef frameDelay, ByRef frameCount, ByRef yPos)
    Call PAL_PushScreen(screen_buf(0))
    For i = 0 To frameCount
        tmp = i Mod 6
        If i < 6 Then
            Call PAL_AddPic0(screen_buf(0), global_buf_1(0), yPos, rng_anim_frames(tmp))
        Else
            Call PAL_AddPic(screen_buf(0), global_buf_1(0), yPos, rng_anim_frames(tmp))
        End If
        Call PAL_PopScreen6a(screen_buf(0), rng_anim_frames(tmp), yPos)
        Call PAL_WaitTime(frameDelay)
        Call update_shake()
    Next
    Call PAL_PopScreen(global_buf_1(0))
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub query_midi_status()
    tmp4 = tmp
    tmp6 = kernel32.mciSendStringA('status midi mode', tmp, 30, 0)
    tmp2 = tmp6
    If Left(tmp, 7) = 'playing' Then
        retval = 1
        Exit Sub
    End If
    retval = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub animate_battle_sprites(ByRef startIdx, ByRef endIdx)
    For i = 1 To 15
        Call PAL_WaitTime(2)
        For j = startIdx To endIdx
            k = mgo_frame_offsets(player_battle_sprite(j).spriteBase + player_battle_sprite(j).direction) + player_battle_sprite(j).spriteBase
            l = player_battle_sprite(j).x - (mgo_frame_offsets(k) \ 2)
            frameData = player_battle_sprite(j).y - mgo_frame_offsets(k + 1)
            Call PAL_PutP(l, frameData, mgo_frame_offsets(k), screen_buffer_ptr, i, 1)
        Next
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub query_cd_status()
    tmp4 = tmp
    tmp6 = kernel32.mciSendStringA('status cdtrack mode', tmp, 30, 0)
    tmp2 = tmp6
    If Left(tmp, 7) = 'playing' Then
        retval = 1
        Exit Sub
    End If
    retval = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub get_scene_map_source(ByRef sceneID)
    If scenes(sceneID).mapNum <> curr_scene_id_cache Then
        filename = 'MAP.MKF'
        Call read_file_and_close(filename, scenes(sceneID).mapNum)
        Call PAL_Unpak(global_buf_2(0), map_data_buf(0))
        filename = 'GOP.MKF'
        result = open_file(filename, 0)
        tmp_file_size = get_subfile_len(result, scenes(sceneID).mapNum)
        Call kernel32._hread(result, mgo_frame_offsets(0), tmp_file_size)
        Call kernel32._lclose(result)
    End If
    curr_scene_id_cache = scenes(sceneID).mapNum
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub player_attack_anim(ByRef roleIdx, ByRef hasEffect)
    player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).direction = 0
    retval = 4
    For i = 1 To 4
        player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).x = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).x - retval
        player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).y = player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).y - (retval \ 2)
        Call draw_battle_scene(1, 0)
        retval = retval - 1
    Next
    Call draw_battle_scene(2, 0)
    player_battle_sprite(roleIdx).direction = 5
    If hasEffect Then
        Call play_sound(playerRoles(party(roleIdx).role, 72), 1)
        Call play_battle_effect(roleIdx)
    End If
    Call draw_battle_scene(1, 0)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub draw_effect_sprites(ByRef useOverlay)
    frameIdx = fire_mkf_data(effect_frame_count)
    For i = 1 To effect_particle_count
        y = effect_x_coords(i) - (fire_mkf_data(frameIdx) \ 2)
        If useOverlay Then
            x = (effect_y_coords(i) - fire_mkf_data(frameIdx + 1)) - battle_y_offset
            bufPtr = PAL_ArrayPtr(global_buf_1(0))
            Call PAL_PutP(y, x, fire_mkf_data(frameIdx), bufPtr, 0, 0)
        Else
            Call PAL_AddToTree(y, (effect_y_coords(i) + effect_frames(i)) - battle_y_offset, effect_frames(i), fire_mkf_data(frameIdx))
        End If
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub player_attack_execute(ByRef roleIdx, ByRef targetRole)
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(battle_order_array(0), 25)
    If player_status(roleIdx, 8) > 0 Then
        retval = 1
    Else
        retval = 0
    End If
    For i = 0 To retval
        Call player_attack_all(roleIdx, retval)
    Next
    Call reset_battle_sprite_pos(roleIdx)
    Call draw_battle_scene(1, 0)
    Call apply_enemy_poison_damage()
    rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
    playerExp(targetRole, 1).count = CSng (playerExp(targetRole, 1).count) + (rng * CSng (2))
    playerExp(targetRole, 3).count = playerExp(targetRole, 3).count + 1
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub cd_stopplay()
    If music_mode = 2 Then
        tmp4 = tmp
        tmp7 = kernel32.mciSendStringA('stop cdtrack', tmp, 30, 0)
        tmp2 = tmp7
        tmp5 = tmp
        tmp7 = kernel32.mciSendStringA('close cdtrack', tmp, 30, 0)
        tmp2 = tmp7
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub midi_close()
    If (music_mode <> 0) And (midi_active <> 0) Then
        midi_playing = 0
        midi_active = 0
        tmp4 = tmp
        tmp7 = kernel32.mciSendStringA('stop midi', tmp, 30, 0)
        tmp2 = tmp7
        tmp5 = tmp
        tmp7 = kernel32.mciSendStringA('close midi', tmp, 30, 0)
        tmp2 = tmp7
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub render_scene_and_fade(fadeSpeed)
    bufPtr = PAL_ArrayPtr(global_buf_1(0))
    Call PAL_ClearClipNA(relative_viewport_x, relative_viewport_y, viewport_offset, viewport_height, viewport_width, bufPtr, global_buf_2(0))
    RPG_screen_wave_grade = RPG_screen_wave_grade + wave_progression
    If (RPG_screen_wave_grade > 0) And (RPG_screen_wave_grade < 256) Then
        Call PAL_RripA(viewport_offset, RPG_screen_wave_grade, global_buf_2(0))
    Else
        RPG_screen_wave_grade = 0
        wave_progression = 0
    End If
    Call PAL_NTree(relative_viewport_x, global_buf_2(0))
    If sprite_frame_count Then
        Call PAL_NipWB(sprite_frame_count, viewport_width, global_buf_2(0))
    Else
        Call PAL_NipWA(frame_counter, viewport_width, global_buf_2(0))
    End If
    Call PAL_WaitTime(10)
    Call fade_out_palette(fadeSpeed)
    Call update_shake()
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub init_walk_frames()
    retval = playerRoles(party(0).role, 64)
    If retval = 0 Then
        retval = 3
    End If
    party(0).frame = RPG_team_direction * retval
    For i = 1 To RPG_team_number
        retval = playerRoles(party(i).role, 64)
        If retval = 0 Then
            retval = 3
        End If
        party(i).frame = trail(2).direction * retval
    Next
    For i = 1 To RPG_other_peoples
        party(RPG_team_number + i).frame = trail(2 + i).direction * 3
    Next
    scene_flags = (scene_flags And 2) Xor 2
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub stop_all_and_exit()
    If init_done_flag = 1 Then
        Call user32.StopApp(1)
        If midi_active = 1 Then
            tmp4 = tmp
            tmp7 = kernel32.mciSendStringA('stop midi', tmp, 30, 0)
            tmp2 = tmp7
        End If
        If cd_active = 1 Then
            tmp5 = tmp
            tmp7 = kernel32.mciSendStringA('stop cdtrack', tmp, 30, 0)
            tmp2 = tmp7
        End If
    End If
    exit_flag = 1
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub npc_walk_one_step(ByRef npcIdx, ByRef stepCount)
    npc_display_data(npcIdx).x = npc_display_data(npcIdx).x + (key_pressed_flags(npc_display_data(npcIdx).direction) * (stepCount + stepCount))
    npc_display_data(npcIdx).y = npc_display_data(npcIdx).y + (key_direction_flags(npc_display_data(npcIdx).direction) * stepCount)
    retval = npc_display_data(npcIdx).spriteFrames
    If retval > 0 Then
        If retval = 3 Then
            retval = 4
        End If
        npc_display_data(npcIdx).currentFrame = (npc_display_data(npcIdx).currentFrame + 1) Mod retval
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If npc_display_data(npcIdx).spriteFramesAuto > 0 Then
        npc_display_data(npcIdx).currentFrame = (npc_display_data(npcIdx).currentFrame + 1) Mod npc_display_data(npcIdx).spriteFramesAuto
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub render_scene_with_rng()
    If multi_event_state = 0 Then
        Call PAL_ExMap(RPG_viewport_x, RPG_viewport_y, map_data_buf(0), global_buf_2(0), mgo_frame_offsets(0))
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If multi_event_state = 2 Then
        If multi_event_param <= 0 Then
            multi_event_param = 1
        End If
        If (multi_event_param_2 Mod multi_event_param) = 0 Then
            ratio = multi_event_param_2 \ multi_event_param
            If ratio < 96 Then
                tmp = 10668
                tmp2 = ratio Mod 6
                If ratio < 6 Then
                    Call PAL_AddPic0(global_buf_1(0), mgo_frame_offsets(0), tmp, rng_anim_frames(tmp2))
                    GoTo L_004090CA
                End If
                Call PAL_AddPic(global_buf_1(0), mgo_frame_offsets(0), tmp, rng_anim_frames(tmp2))
                GoTo L_004090CA
            End If
            multi_event_state = 1
        End If
L_004090CA:
        multi_event_param_2 = multi_event_param_2 + 1
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub remove_inventory_item(ByRef itemID, ByRef amount)
    found = amount
    For i = 0 To 255
        If ((inventory(i).item = itemID) And (inventory(i).amount > 0)) And (found > 0) Then
            inventory(i).amountInUse = inventory(i).amountInUse - found
            If inventory(i).amountInUse < 0 Then
                inventory(i).amountInUse = 0
            End If
            If inventory(i).amount >= found Then
                inventory(i).amount = inventory(i).amount - found
                found = 0
            Else
                found = found - inventory(i).amount
                inventory(i).amount = 0
                inventory(i).item = 0
            End If
        End If
    Next
    Call unequip_item(itemID, found)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub scene_transition()
L_00409288:
    flag_battling = 0
    scene_enter_flag = 0
    scene_leave_flag = 0
    If redraw_flag And 32 Then
        Call LoadRPG_internal(rpg_to_load)
        GoTo L_004092F6
    End If
    If RPG_curr_scene <> scene_to_load Then
        RPG_screen_wave_grade = 0
        wave_progression = 0
        If RPG_curr_scene > 0 Then
            Call load_event_objects()
        End If
    End If
L_004092F6:
    multi_event_state = 0
    team_abstract_x = 160
    team_abstract_y = 112
    RPG_curr_scene = scene_to_load
    If redraw_flag And 4 Then
        Call load_scene_events(RPG_curr_scene)
    End If
    Call get_scene_map_source(RPG_curr_scene)
    Call load_npc_sprites()
    Call draw_battle_row_sprite()
    If redraw_flag And 1 Then
        Call load_team_mgo()
    End If
    If redraw_flag And 8 Then
        redraw_flag = redraw_flag And 2
        Call process_Script(0, scenes(RPG_curr_scene).scriptOnEnter)
        If RPG_curr_scene <> scene_to_load Then
            GoTo L_00409288
        End If
    End If
    If redraw_flag And 2 Then
        Call play_all_kinds_music(RPG_music_number, 1)
    End If
    redraw_flag = 0
    Call init_battle_state()
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub sort_player_magic(ByRef roleID)
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(battle_order_array(0), 150)
    retval = -1
    For i = 32 To 63
        If playerRoles(roleID, i) > 0 Then
            retval = retval + 1
            battle_order_array(retval) = playerRoles(roleID, i)
        End If
    Next
    For i = 0 To (retval - 1)
        For j = (i + 1) To retval
            If battle_order_array(i) > battle_order_array(j) Then
                Call swap_values(battle_order_array(i), battle_order_array(j))
            End If
        Next
    Next
    For i = 0 To 31
        playerRoles(roleID, i + 32) = battle_order_array(i)
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub walk_party_fastest(ByRef startFrame, ByRef endFrame, ByRef speed)
    floatVal = CSng (100) / CSng (speed)
    Call PAL_CopyMem(bg_buf(0), global_buf_1(0), 64000)
    For i = startFrame To endFrame
        AryLock mem=lockMem
        Call PAL_CopyMem(file_offset_table(0), rng_anim_data(i * 4), 8)
        AryUnlock mem=lockMem
        tmp_file_size = file_offset_table(1) - file_offset_table(0)
        If tmp_file_size > 0 Then
            AryLock mem=lockMem
            Call PAL_Unpak(rng_anim_data(file_offset_table(0)), global_buf_1(0))
            AryUnlock mem=lockMem
            Call PAL_RngPut(global_buf_1(0))
            Call PAL_WaitTime(floatVal)
            Call fade_out_palette(1)
            Call update_shake()
        Else
            Exit For
        End If
    Next
    Call PAL_CopyMem(global_buf_1(0), bg_buf(0), 64000)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub play_cd(ByRef trackNum, musicNum, ByRef trackValid)
    If music_mode = 2 Then
        Call midi_close()
        Call cd_stop()
        If use_cd_flag Then
            If trackNum > 0 Then
                trackStr = VB40032.rtcStrFromVar(trackNum)
                cmdStr2 = 'play cdtrack from' & VB40032.rtcStrFromVar(trackNum)
                tmp3 = ('play cdtrack from' & VB40032.rtcStrFromVar(trackNum)) & ' to'
                trackStr2 = VB40032.rtcStrFromVar(trackNum + 1)
                cmdStr = (('play cdtrack from' & VB40032.rtcStrFromVar(trackNum)) & ' to') & VB40032.rtcStrFromVar(trackNum + 1)
                tmp3 = tmp
                mciResult = kernel32.mciSendStringA(cmdStr, tmp, 30, 0)
                tmp2 = mciResult
                cd_track_valid = trackValid
                cd_active = 1
                cd_track_num = trackNum
            End If
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Call play_all_kinds_music(musicNum, trackValid)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub compact_inventory()
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(battle_order_array(0), 256)
    foundIdx = -1
    For i = 0 To 255
        If inventory(i).amount > 99 Then
            inventory(i).amount = 99
        End If
        If inventory(i).amount > 0 Then
            foundIdx = foundIdx + 1
            battle_order_array(foundIdx) = i
        End If
    Next
    For i = 0 To foundIdx
        Call PAL_CopyMem(k(0), inventory(i), 6)
        Call PAL_CopyMem(inventory(i), inventory(battle_order_array(i)), 6)
        Call PAL_CopyMem(inventory(battle_order_array(i)), k(0), 6)
    Next
    retval = foundIdx
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub init_battle_state()
    For i = 0 To 255
        inventory(i).amountInUse = 0
    Next
    Call PAL_ClearMenu(equipment_effect(0, 11, 17), 490)
    For i = 0 To RPG_team_number
        k = party(i).role
        playerRoles(k, 4) = 0
        player_battle_sprite(i).spriteNum = playerRoles(k, 1)
        player_battle_sprite(i).equipID = playerRoles(k, 65)
        player_status(i, 8) = 0
        For j = 11 To 16
            If playerRoles(k, j) > 0 Then
                Call process_Script(i, objects(playerRoles(k, j)).data3)
            End If
        Next
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub yes_no_menu(ByRef optionFlags, ByRef labelIdx)
    menuX = 120
    menuY = 100
    selection = -1
    isYes = optionFlags And 1
    inputResult = -2
    While inputResult = -2
        tmpX = menuX
        For i = 0 To 1
            Call frame_menu(tmpX, menuY, 2, selection)
            If i = isYes Then
                Call draw_text_at(tmpX + 15, menuY + 9, labelIdx + i, 250)
            Else
                Call draw_string(tmpX + 16, menuY + 10, word_dat_data(labelIdx + i), 3, 0)
            End If
            tmpX = tmpX + 80
        Next
        selection = 0
        keyResult = read_key()
        If keyResult = 1 Then
            inputResult = -1
        End If
        If keyResult = 2 Then
            inputResult = isYes
        End If
        If (keyResult = 5) Or (keyResult = 6) Then
            isYes = 1 - isYes
        End If
    Wend
    retval = inputResult
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub select_enemy_target()
    If count_alive_enemies() <= 1 Then
        i = 0
        For j = 0 To team_number
            If enemy_battle_data(j).hp > 0 Then
                i = j
            End If
        Next
    Else
        multi_event_param_3 = 0
        i = -2
        k = 1
        While i = -2
            key_pressed = check_key_pressed()
            If key_pressed = 5 Then
                multi_event_param_3 = multi_event_param_3 - 1
                k = -1
            End If
            If key_pressed = 6 Then
                multi_event_param_3 = multi_event_param_3 + 1
                k = 1
            End If
            If multi_event_param_3 < 0 Then
                multi_event_param_3 = team_number
            End If
            If multi_event_param_3 > team_number Then
                multi_event_param_3 = 0
            End If
L_00409EEC:
            If enemy_battle_data(multi_event_param_3).hp <= 0 Then
                multi_event_param_3 = ((multi_event_param_3 + k) + enemy_max_id) Mod enemy_max_id
                GoTo L_00409EEC
            End If
            If key_pressed = 1 Then
                i = -1
            End If
            If key_pressed = 2 Then
                i = multi_event_param_3
            End If
            Call draw_battle_scene(1, 0)
        Wend
        multi_event_param_3 = -1
    End If
    retval = i
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub get_sprites_curr_scene()
    For i = 1 To curr_scene_event_count
        If npc_display_data(i).state > 0 Then
            npcX = npc_display_data(i).x - RPG_viewport_x
            npcY = npc_display_data(i).y - RPG_viewport_y
            layerVal = npc_display_data(i).layer * 8
            If (((npcX >= -64) And (npcY >= 0)) And (npcX <= 384)) And (npcY <= 328) Then
                spriteNum = npc_display_data(i).currentFrame
                If npc_display_data(i).spriteFrames = 3 Then
                    If spriteNum = 2 Then
                        spriteNum = 0
                    End If
                    If spriteNum = 3 Then
                        spriteNum = 2
                    End If
                    frameCount = npc_display_data(i).direction * 3
                Else
                    frameCount = npc_display_data(i).direction * npc_display_data(i).spriteFrames
                End If
                If npc_display_data(i).spriteNum > 0 Then
                    Call add_sprite_to_tree(npcX, npcY, layerVal, i, frameCount + spriteNum, 1)
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub menu_system()
    retval = menu_loop(tmp, 40, 60, 11, 4, 5)
    tmp2 = retval
    If tmp2 = 0 Then
        result = check_save_file()
        If result >= 0 Then
            Call SaveRPG_internal(result + 1)
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If tmp2 = 1 Then
        result = check_save_file()
        If result >= 0 Then
            Call LoadRPG_internal(result + 1)
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If tmp2 = 2 Then
        result = use_cd_flag
        result2 = yes_no_menu(result, 17)
        If result2 >= 0 Then
            use_cd_flag = result2
            If use_cd_flag = 0 Then
                Call cd_stop()
                Call midi_close()
                Exit Sub
            End If
            Call play_all_kinds_music(music_track_arg, music_mode_arg)
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If tmp2 = 3 Then
        result = has_sfx
        result2 = yes_no_menu(result, 17)
        If result2 >= 0 Then
            has_sfx = result2
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If tmp2 = 4 Then
        result = 0
        result2 = yes_no_menu(result, 19)
        If result2 = 1 Then
            Call release_resources_exit()
        End If
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub select_party_member()
    Call push_screen_buffer()
    bufPtr = PAL_ArrayPtr(global_buf_1(0))
    inputResult = bufPtr
    Call PAL_CopyMem(global_buf_2(0), menu_bg_data(0), menu_bg_size)
    selection = -2
    x = 158
    highlight = 0
    selectedIndex = 0
    While selection = -2
        If selectedIndex > RPG_team_number Then
            selectedIndex = RPG_team_number
        End If
        If selectedIndex < 0 Then
            selectedIndex = 0
        End If
        y = 75 + (selectedIndex * 80)
        Call PAL_CopyMem(global_buf_1(0), screen_buf(0), 64000)
        Call PAL_PutP(y, x, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(67)), inputResult, 0, 0)
        Call PAL_PopScreen(global_buf_1(0))
        keyResult = read_key()
        If keyResult = 5 Then
            selectedIndex = selectedIndex - 1
        End If
        If keyResult = 6 Then
            selectedIndex = selectedIndex + 1
        End If
        If keyResult = 1 Then
            selection = -1
            selectedIndex = -1
        End If
        If keyResult = 2 Then
            selection = selectedIndex
        End If
    Wend
    Call restore_screen()
    retval = selectedIndex
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub play_theurgy_anim(magicIdx, ByRef startFrame, ByRef minFrames)
    Call copy_subfile_data(magicIdx)
    If minFrames < 9 Then
        Call play_sound(theurgy_data(0).sound, 0)
    End If
    theurgy_effect_max = theurgy_data(0).wave
    For i = 0 To theurgy_data(0).effectTimes
        effect_frame_count = startFrame
        While fire_mkf_data(effect_frame_count) > 0
            Call draw_battle_scene(1, theurgy_data(0).speed)
            effect_frame_count = effect_frame_count + 1
        Wend
    Next
    effect_frame_count = effect_frame_count - 1
    battle_y_offset = 16
    j = 10
    For i = 1 To theurgy_data(0).shake
        Call draw_battle_scene(1, 0)
        j = 10 - j
        battle_y_offset = Int (VB40032.rtcRandomNext() * CSng (6)) + CSng (j)
    Next
    battle_y_offset = 0
    theurgy_effect_max = 0
    If theurgy_data(0).keepEffect And (theurgy_effect_count < 9) Then
        Call draw_effect_sprites(-1)
        Call PAL_CopyMem(bg_buf(0), global_buf_1(0), 64000)
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub show_party_hp_mp_change(ByRef thyrgyType)
    For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
        roleID = party(i).role
        hpDiff = playerRoles(roleID, 9) - battle_role_data_ext(i).savedHP
        If hpDiff <> 0 Then
            If hpDiff > 0 Then
                j = 0
            Else
                j = 1
            End If
            Call add_damage_number(player_battle_sprite(i).x, player_battle_sprite(i).y - 70, Abs (hpDiff), j)
            thyrgyType = -1
        End If
        hpDiff = playerRoles(roleID, 10) - battle_role_data_ext(i).savedMP
        If hpDiff > 0 Then
            Call add_damage_number(player_battle_sprite(i).x, player_battle_sprite(i).y - 62, hpDiff, 2)
            thyrgyType = -1
        End If
        If playerRoles(roleID, 9) < 0 Then
            playerRoles(roleID, 9) = 0
        End If
        If playerRoles(roleID, 10) < 0 Then
            playerRoles(roleID, 10) = 0
        End If
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub load_npc_sprites()
    dup = 0
    For i = 1 To curr_scene_event_count
        spriteNum = npc_display_data(i).spriteNum
        If spriteNum > 0 Then
            found = -1
            For j = 1 To (i - 1)
                If spriteNum = npc_display_data(j).spriteNum Then
                    found = j
                End If
            Next
            If found = -1 Then
                Call load_subfile_to_buf(spriteNum)
                bufPtr = PAL_PakSize(global_buf_2(0))
                tmp = bufPtr
                If (dup + tmp) > 0 Then
                    Call PAL_Unpak(global_buf_2(0), fire_mkf_data(dup))
                    npc_display_data(i).spritePtrOffset = dup
                    dup = dup + tmp
                    GoTo L_0040AA68
                End If
                npc_display_data(i).spriteNum = 0
                GoTo L_0040AA68
            End If
            npc_display_data(i).spritePtrOffset = npc_display_data(found).spritePtrOffset
        End If
L_0040AA68:
        overlap = 0
        While fire_mkf_data(npc_display_data(i).spritePtrOffset + overlap) > 0
            overlap = overlap + 1
        Wend
        npc_display_data(i).spriteFramesAuto = overlap
        If overlap = 0 Then
            npc_display_data(i).spriteNum = 0
        End If
    Next
    battle_extra_flag = dup
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub stop_app_and_music()
    Call user32.StopApp(0)
    If midi_active = 1 Then
        tmp5 = tmp
        tmp8 = kernel32.mciSendStringA('play midi', tmp, 30, 0)
        tmp3 = tmp8
    End If
    If cd_active = 1 Then
        tmp2 = (('play cdtrack from' + CStr(cd_track_num)) + ' to') + CStr(cd_track_num + 1)
        tmp6 = tmp
        tmp8 = kernel32.mciSendStringA(tmp2, tmp, 30, 0)
        tmp3 = tmp8
    End If
    exit_flag = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub show_equip_detail(ByRef roleID, equipIdx)
    Call frame_menu(78, 0, 10, -1)
    name1 = trim_string(word_dat_data(playerRoles(roleID, 3)))
    name2 = trim_string(word_dat_data(48))
    tmp = name1 & name2
    name3 = trim_string(word_dat_data(32))
    name = (name1 & name2) & name3
    Call draw_string(102, 8, name, 3, 0)
    Call draw_menu_table(72, 34, 9, 10, 8, -1)
    Call show_role_attributes(88, 47, roleID, -1)
    Call level_up_player(roleID, equipIdx, -1)
    Call show_role_attributes(169, 47, roleID, 0)
    Call wait_frame(200)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub use_item_menu()
    Call show_status_icons()
    i = menu_select_party(36, 64, -1)
    If i >= 0 Then
L_0040AF7C:
        j = party(i).role
        itemID = select_thurgy(j, menuX, 1)
        If itemID < 0 Then
            Exit Sub
        End If
        menuY = objects(itemID).data0
        Call PAL_GetBin(cursorPos, objects(itemID).data6, 4)
        If cursorPos = 0 Then
            selection = select_party_member()
            If selection < 0 Then
                GoTo L_0040AF7C
            End If
        Else
            selection = j
        End If
        For keyResult = 0 To RPG_team_number
            tmp = tmp + playerRoles(keyResult, 9)
        Next
        Call process_Script(selection, objects(itemID).data2)
        Call copy_subfile_data(menuY)
        If redraw_hp_mp_flag Then
            playerRoles(j, 10) = playerRoles(j, 10) - theurgy_data(0).costMP
        End If
        If palette_fade_active = 0 Then
            For keyResult = 0 To RPG_team_number
                tmp = tmp - playerRoles(keyResult, 9)
            Next
            If tmp <> 0 Then
                Call show_status_icons()
                GoTo L_0040AF7C
            End If
        End If
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub load_team_mgo()
    i = 0
    For j = 0 To RPG_team_number
        roleID = playerRoles(party(j).role, 2)
        l = -1
        For k = 0 To (j - 1)
            If roleID = playerRoles(party(k).role, 2) Then
                l = k
            End If
        Next
        If l = -1 Then
            Call load_subfile_to_buf(roleID)
            bufPtr = PAL_PakSize(global_buf_2(0))
            found = bufPtr
            Call PAL_Unpak(global_buf_2(0), word_glyph_index(i))
            party(j).imageOffset = i
            i = i + found
        Else
            party(j).imageOffset = party(l).imageOffset
        End If
    Next
    For k = 1 To RPG_other_peoples
        j = RPG_team_number + k
        Call load_subfile_to_buf(party(j).role)
        bufPtr = PAL_PakSize(global_buf_2(0))
        found = bufPtr
        Call PAL_Unpak(global_buf_2(0), word_glyph_index(i))
        party(j).imageOffset = i
        i = i + found
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub load_enemy_sprites()
    i = 0
    For j = 0 To team_number
        If (enemy_battle_data(j).hp > 0) And (enemy_battle_data(j).objectID > 0) Then
            l = enemy_battle_data(j).enemyID
            spriteIdx = -1
            For k = 0 To (j - 1)
                If enemy_battle_data(k).hp > 0 Then
                    If l = enemy_battle_data(k).enemyID Then
                        spriteIdx = k
                    End If
                End If
            Next
            If spriteIdx = -1 Then
                tmp_file_size = get_subfile_len(fh_ABC_MKF, l)
                Call kernel32._hread(fh_ABC_MKF, global_buf_2(0), tmp_file_size)
                bufPtr = PAL_PakSize(global_buf_2(0))
                found = bufPtr
                Call PAL_Unpak(global_buf_2(0), map_data_buf(i))
                enemy_battle_data(j).tileData = map_data_buf((map_data_buf(i) + i) + 1)
                enemy_battle_data(j).mapValue = i
                i = i + found
            Else
                enemy_battle_data(j).tileData = enemy_battle_data(spriteIdx).tileData
                enemy_battle_data(j).mapValue = enemy_battle_data(spriteIdx).mapValue
            End If
        End If
    Next
    Call init_enemy_positions()
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub scroll_scene_with_fbp(fbpSubfile, ByRef rngSubfile, ByRef speed)
    stepDelay = CSng (100) / CSng (speed)
    Call load_fbp_two_scene(fbpSubfile)
    If rngSubfile > 0 Then
        Call load_subfile_to_buf(rngSubfile)
        Call PAL_Unpak(global_buf_2(0), fire_mkf_data(0))
        frameIdx = 0
    End If
    frameCount = 200
    i = 0
    For j = 1 To 200
        frameCount = frameCount - 1
        If frameCount >= 0 Then
            bufPtr = PAL_ArrayPtr(global_buf_1(0))
            offset = bufPtr + (frameCount * 320)
        End If
        Call PAL_ClearClipNA(0, 0, viewport_offset, viewport_height, viewport_offset, offset, global_buf_2(0))
        If rngSubfile <> 0 Then
            Call PAL_PutIpNA(0, 0, 0, viewport_offset, fire_mkf_data(fire_mkf_data(frameIdx)), global_buf_2(0))
            frameIdx = frameIdx + 1
            If fire_mkf_data(frameIdx) = 0 Then
                frameIdx = 0
            End If
        End If
        If palette_fade_active And (i <= 64) Then
            i = i + 1
            Call PAL_ExPalate(palette_data(768), palette_data(0), 768, i)
            Call PAL_IntPalate(palette_data(768))
        End If
        Call PAL_NipWA(0, viewport_offset, global_buf_2(0))
        Call PAL_WaitTime(stepDelay)
    Next
    palette_fade_active = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub play_theurgy_rng_anim()
    Call PAL_CopyMem(global_buf_1(0), bg_buf(0), 64000)
    If npc_dir_frame And (theurgy_data(0).effectTimes <> 0) Then
        Call PAL_PopScreenB(global_buf_1(0), theurgy_data(0).effectTimes)
    End If
    Call draw_enemy_battle_frame()
    Call PAL_PushScreen(fire_mkf_data(0))
    For i = 0 To 46
        tmp = i Mod 6
        If i < 6 Then
            Call PAL_AddPic0(fire_mkf_data(0), global_buf_1(0), 10668, rng_anim_frames(tmp))
        Else
            Call PAL_AddPic(fire_mkf_data(0), global_buf_1(0), 10668, rng_anim_frames(tmp))
            Call PAL_AddPic(fire_mkf_data(0), global_buf_1(0), 10668, rng_anim_frames(tmp))
            Call PAL_PopScreen6(fire_mkf_data(0), rng_anim_frames(tmp))
        End If
        Call PAL_WaitTime(2)
    Next
    Call PAL_CopyMem(global_buf_1(0), bg_buf(0), 64000)
    If npc_dir_frame And (theurgy_data(0).effectTimes <> 0) Then
        Call PAL_PopScreenB(global_buf_1(0), theurgy_data(0).effectTimes)
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub add_magic_to_player(ByRef roleID, ByRef magicID, ByRef magicType)
    retval = -1
    foundIdx = 0
    For i = 63 To 32 Step -1
        If playerRoles(roleID, i) = magicID Then
            retval = 0
        End If
        If playerRoles(roleID, i) = 0 Then
            foundIdx = i
        End If
    Next
    If retval And (foundIdx > 0) Then
        playerRoles(roleID, foundIdx) = magicID
        If magicType Then
            Call frame_menu(60, 100, 11, -1)
            Call draw_string(70, 110, word_dat_data(playerRoles(roleID, 3)), 3, 0)
            Call draw_string(118, 110, word_dat_data(33), 3, 0)
            Call draw_string(158, 110, word_dat_data(magicID), 3, 25)
            Call wait_frame(180)
        End If
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub menu_select(ByRef cursorPos, ByRef y, ByRef x, ByRef rowCount, ByRef colCount)
    maxCol = rowCount - 1
    inputResult = colCount - 1
    Call draw_menu_table(y, x, 0, rowCount, colCount, -1)
    selection = -2
    While selection = -2
        If cursorPos < 0 Then
            cursorPos = inputResult
        End If
        If cursorPos > inputResult Then
            cursorPos = 0
        End If
        cursorPos = x + 12
        tmp = y + 13
        For i = 0 To inputResult
            If battle_order_array(i + 100) Then
                If (i = cursorPos) And (selection = -2) Then
                    j = 250
                    GoTo L_0040BD8E
                End If
                j = 78
                GoTo L_0040BD8E
            End If
            If (i = cursorPos) And (selection = -2) Then
                j = 28
                GoTo L_0040BD8E
            End If
            j = 24
L_0040BD8E:
            Call draw_text_at(tmp, cursorPos, battle_order_array(i), j)
            cursorPos = cursorPos + 18
        Next
        j = read_key()
        If j = 1 Then
            selection = -1
        End If
        If (j = 2) And battle_order_array(cursorPos + 100) Then
            selection = cursorPos
            Call draw_text_at(y + 13, (x + 12) + (selection * 18), battle_order_array(selection), 43)
        End If
        If j = 3 Then
            cursorPos = cursorPos - 1
        End If
        If j = 4 Then
            cursorPos = cursorPos + 1
        End If
    Wend
    maxRow = selection
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub draw_player_status(ByRef x, ByRef y, ByRef roleID, ByRef highlight)
    retval = global_buf_2(48 + roleID)
    If highlight = 0 Then
        Call PAL_PutP(x - 3, y, global_buf_2(retval), screen_buffer_ptr, 0, 0)
    Else
        Call PAL_PutP(x - 3, y, global_buf_2(retval), screen_buffer_ptr, highlight, 2)
    End If
    Call PAL_PutP(x + 47, y + 11, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(39)), screen_buffer_ptr, 0, 0)
    Call PAL_PutP(x + 47, y + 27, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(39)), screen_buffer_ptr, 0, 0)
    Call display_number(x + 64, y + 13, playerRoles(roleID, 7), 0)
    Call display_number(x + 64, y + 29, playerRoles(roleID, 8), 2)
    Call display_number(x + 42, y + 9, playerRoles(roleID, 9), 0)
    Call display_number(x + 42, y + 25, playerRoles(roleID, 10), 2)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub draw_menu_table(x, ByRef y, ByRef wordIdx, ByRef rowCount, ByRef colCount, ByRef highlight)
    If rowCount < 0 Then
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Call PAL_CopyMem(global_buf_2(0), menu_bg_data(0), menu_bg_size)
    i = colCount - 2
    j = rowCount - 2
    menuX = y
    save_temp_buf(0) = wordIdx
    menuY = 1
    For k = 0 To j
        save_temp_buf(menuY) = wordIdx + 1
        menuY = menuY + 1
    Next
    save_temp_buf(menuY) = wordIdx + 2
    Call draw_menu_frame(x, menuX, menuY, highlight)
    menuX = menuX + 20
    For l = 0 To i
        save_temp_buf(0) = wordIdx + 3
        menuY = 1
        For k = 0 To j
            save_temp_buf(menuY) = wordIdx + 4
            menuY = menuY + 1
        Next
        save_temp_buf(menuY) = wordIdx + 5
        Call draw_menu_frame(x, menuX, menuY, highlight)
        menuX = menuX + 18
    Next
    save_temp_buf(0) = wordIdx + 6
    menuY = 1
    For k = 0 To j
        save_temp_buf(menuY) = wordIdx + 7
        menuY = menuY + 1
    Next
    save_temp_buf(menuY) = wordIdx + 8
    Call draw_menu_frame(x, menuX, menuY, -1 And highlight)
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub draw_role_battle_frame()
    For i = enemy_pos_count To 0 Step -1
        frameIdx = mgo_frame_offsets(player_battle_sprite(i).spriteBase + player_battle_sprite(i).direction) + player_battle_sprite(i).spriteBase
        y = player_battle_sprite(i).x - (mgo_frame_offsets(frameIdx) \ 2)
        If (player_battle_sprite(i).direction = 0) And player_status(i, 0) Then
            x = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
        Else
            x = 0
        End If
        If battle_select_max = 0 Then
            Call PAL_AddToTree(y, (player_battle_sprite(i).y - x) + player_battle_sprite(i).animOffset, player_battle_sprite(i).animOffset, mgo_frame_offsets(frameIdx))
        End If
        If battle_role_idx_2 Then
            If i = battle_target_cursor Then
                Call PAL_AddToTree(player_battle_sprite(i).x - 8, player_battle_sprite(i).y, 61, word_glyph_index(word_glyph_index(66 + (effect_sub_count And 1))))
            End If
            If i = battle_curr_role_idx Then
                Call PAL_AddToTree(player_battle_sprite(i).x - 8, player_battle_sprite(i).y, 68, word_glyph_index(word_glyph_index(68 + (effect_sub_count And 1))))
            End If
        End If
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub load_enemy_data(ByRef enemyIdx, ByRef objectID)
    enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).objectID = objectID
    If objectID > 0 Then
        enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).enemyID = objects(objectID).data0
        Call PAL_CopyMem(enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).idleFrames, data_enemy(enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).enemyID * 70), 70)
        enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).hp = enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).health
        enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).useScript = objects(objectID).data2
        enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).battleStartScript = objects(objectID).data3
        enemy_battle_data(enemyIdx).battleEndScript = objects(objectID).data4
        If enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).exp > (32767 - battle_max_hp_sum) Then
            battle_max_hp_sum = 32767
        Else
            battle_max_hp_sum = battle_max_hp_sum + enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).exp
        End If
        battle_action_type = battle_action_type + enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).cash
        Call PAL_LoadDSound(enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).attackSound, 0)
        Call PAL_LoadDSound(enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).actionSound, 0)
        Call PAL_LoadDSound(enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).magicSound, 0)
        Call PAL_LoadDSound(enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).deathSound, 0)
        Call PAL_LoadDSound(enemy_runtime_data(enemyIdx).callSound, 0)
    End If
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub fade_in_pic(fbpSubfile, ByRef rngSubfile, ByRef speed)
    floatVal = CSng (100) / CSng (speed)
    filename = 'FBP.MKF'
    Call read_file_and_close(filename, fbpSubfile)
    Call PAL_Unpak(global_buf_2(0), mgo_frame_offsets(0))
    If rngSubfile > 0 Then
        Call load_subfile_to_buf(rngSubfile)
        Call PAL_Unpak(global_buf_2(0), fire_mkf_data(0))
        frameIdx = 0
    End If
    multi_event_state = 2
    multi_event_param = 1
    multi_event_param_2 = 0
    frameCount = 0
    For i = 1 To 100
        Call render_scene_with_rng()
        Call PAL_ClearClipNA(0, 0, viewport_offset, viewport_height, viewport_offset, bufPtr, global_buf_2(0))
        bufPtr = PAL_ArrayPtr(global_buf_1(0))
        If rngSubfile <> 0 Then
            Call PAL_PutIpNA(0, 0, 0, viewport_offset, fire_mkf_data(fire_mkf_data(frameIdx)), global_buf_2(0))
            frameIdx = frameIdx + 1
            If fire_mkf_data(frameIdx) = 0 Then
                frameIdx = 0
            End If
        End If
        If palette_fade_active And (frameCount <= 64) Then
            frameCount = frameCount + 1
            Call PAL_ExPalate(palette_data(768), palette_data(0), 768, frameCount)
            Call PAL_IntPalate(palette_data(768))
        End If
        Call PAL_NipWA(0, viewport_offset, global_buf_2(0))
        Call PAL_WaitTime(floatVal)
    Next
    palette_fade_active = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub update_player_battle_status()
    count = 0
    For i = 0 To enemy_pos_count
        roleID = party(i).role
        player_battle_sprite(i).direction = player_battle_sprite(i).actionState
        battle_role_action(i).flag = 0
        If (playerRoles(roleID, 9) < 100) And (playerRoles(roleID, 9) <= (playerRoles(roleID, 7) \ 5)) Then
            player_battle_sprite(i).direction = 1
        End If
        If player_status(i, 2) Then
            player_battle_sprite(i).direction = 1
        End If
        If player_status(i, 4) Then
            player_battle_sprite(i).direction = 0
            GoTo L_0040CC0C
        End If
        If playerRoles(roleID, 9) <= 0 Then
            player_battle_sprite(i).direction = 2
            If player_battle_sprite(i).origDirection <> 2 Then
                battle_role_action(i).flag = -1
            End If
        End If
L_0040CC0C:
        player_battle_sprite(i).origDirection = player_battle_sprite(i).direction
        If player_battle_sprite(i).direction <= 1 Then
            player_battle_sprite(i).x = player_battle_sprite(i).origX
            player_battle_sprite(i).y = player_battle_sprite(i).origY
        End If
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub cast_theurgy_anim(ByRef itemID)
    shake_active = -1
    retval = objects(itemID).data0
    Call copy_subfile_data(retval)
    Call play_sound(theurgy_data(0).sound, 0)
    Call draw_text_at(200, 50, itemID, 14)
    attack_done_flag = 0
    Call animate_battle_sprites(0, enemy_pos_count)
    Call read_file_to_buf(theurgy_data(0).layerOffset + 10, 0)
    Call PAL_WaitTime(5)
    curr_npc_idx = 0
    npc_curr_frame = 240 + theurgy_data(0).xOffset
    npc_direction = 165 + theurgy_data(0).yOffset
    Call play_theurgy_rng_anim()
    k = curr_npc_idx
    For i = 1 To 2
        curr_npc_idx = k
        For j = 1 To theurgy_data(0).speed
            Call draw_battle_scene(2, 0)
            curr_npc_idx = curr_npc_idx + 1
        Next
    Next
    For j = 1 To theurgy_data(0).keepEffect
        Call draw_battle_scene(4, 0)
        curr_npc_idx = curr_npc_idx + 1
    Next
    Call load_theurgy_image(theurgy_data(0).effect)
    Call PAL_WaitTime(20)
    For j = 1 To (theurgy_data(0).fireDelay - 1)
        Call draw_battle_scene(1, 0)
        curr_npc_idx = curr_npc_idx + 1
    Next
    shake_active = 0
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub show_role_attributes(ByRef x, ByRef y, ByRef roleID, ByRef showEquip)
    Call display_number(x + 58, y + 4, playerRoles(roleID, 6), 0)
    retval = y
    For i = 48 To 55
        If showEquip Then
            Call draw_text_at(x, retval, i, 187)
        Else
            Call PAL_PutP(x + 16, retval + 6, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(47)), screen_buffer_ptr, 0, 0)
        End If
        retval = retval + 18
    Next
    retval = y + 20
    For i = 7 To 8
        Call display_number(x + 58, retval, playerRoles(roleID, i + 2), 0)
        Call PAL_PutP(x + 62, retval + 3, global_buf_2(global_buf_2(39)), screen_buffer_ptr, 0, 0)
        Call display_number(x + 78, retval + 6, playerRoles(roleID, i), 1)
        retval = retval + 18
    Next
    retval = y + 58
    For i = 17 To 21
        result = get_player_attribute_total(roleID, i)
        Call display_number(x + 58, retval, result, 0)
        retval = retval + 18
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub redraw_tile(ByRef x, ByRef y, ByRef w, ByRef h, ByRef tileX, ByRef tileY)
    Call PAL_VWindow(x, y, w, h)
    tileCol = tileX \ 32
    xPixelOffset = -tileX Mod 32 - 16
    endTileCol = (((tileX + w) - x) + 16) \ 32
    tileRow = tileY \ 16
    yPixelOffset = -tileY Mod 16 - 8
    endTileRow = (((tileY + h) - y) + 8) \ 16
    screenY = y + yPixelOffset
    For i = tileRow To endTileRow
        For j = 0 To 1
            screenX = (x + xPixelOffset) + (j * 16)
            For k = tileCol To endTileCol
                Call PAL_ExGop(map_data_buf(0), j, k, i, tileData, tileData2)
                Call PAL_CvLong(mgo_frame_offsets(tileData))
                bufPtr = PAL_ArrayPtr(global_buf_1(0))
                Call PAL_PutP(screenX, screenY, mgo_frame_offsets(tileFrame), bufPtr, 0, 0)
                If tileData2 > 0 Then
                    Call PAL_CvLong(mgo_frame_offsets(tileData2 - 1))
                    bufPtr = PAL_ArrayPtr(global_buf_1(0))
                    Call PAL_PutP(screenX, screenY, mgo_frame_offsets(tileFrame2), bufPtr, 0, 0)
                End If
                screenX = screenX + 32
            Next
            screenY = screenY + 8
        Next
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub update_enemy_battle_anim()
    For i = team_number To 0 Step -1
        idleSpeed = enemy_runtime_data(i).idleAnimSpeed
        If ((enemy_battle_data(i).hp > 0) Or (battle_enemy_data_ext(i) > 0)) And (idleSpeed > 0) Then
            If attack_done_flag Then
                If (idleSpeed < 99) And ((enemy_status(i, 1) + enemy_status(i, 2)) = 0) Then
                    If (effect_sub_count Mod idleSpeed) = 0 Then
                        enemy_battle_data(i).direction = (enemy_battle_data(i).direction + 1) Mod enemy_runtime_data(i).idleFrames
                    End If
                End If
            End If
            j = map_data_buf(enemy_battle_data(i).mapValue + enemy_battle_data(i).direction) + enemy_battle_data(i).mapValue
            k = enemy_battle_data(i).x - (map_data_buf(j) \ 2)
            If battle_role_idx_2 Then
                If (effect_sub_count And 1) = 1 Then
                    If i = multi_event_param_3 Then
                        player_hit_flags(i) = -1
                    End If
                End If
            End If
            If enemy_status(i, 0) Then
                tmp = VB40032.rtcRandomNext() * CSng (3)
            Else
                tmp = 0
            End If
            Call PAL_AddToTree((k + tmp) + battle_dest_x, (enemy_battle_data(i).y - battle_y_offset) + (battle_dest_x \ 2), 0, map_data_buf(j))
        End If
    Next
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub select_direction_menu(ByRef direction)
    result = -2
    While result = -2
        keyResult = check_key_pressed()
        If keyResult = 3 Then
            direction = 0
        End If
        If (keyResult = 5) And (battle_order_array(1) = 0) Then
            direction = 1
        End If
        If (keyResult = 6) And (battle_order_array(2) = 0) Then
            direction = 2
        End If
        If keyResult = 4 Then
            direction = 3
        End If
        If keyResult = 2 Then
            result = direction
        End If
        If keyResult = 1 Then
            result = -1
        End If
        If keyResult >= 9 Then
            result = -keyResult
        End If
        If (direction > 0) And (direction <= 3) Then
            If battle_order_array(direction) Then
                direction = 0
            End If
        End If
        For i = 0 To 3
            If battle_order_array(i) Then
                Call PAL_PutP(key_scan_map(i), key_action_map(i), word_glyph_index(word_glyph_index(40 + i)), screen_buffer_ptr, 20, 2)
                GoTo L_0040DB3E
            End If
            If i = direction Then
                Call PAL_PutP(key_scan_map(i), key_action_map(i), word_glyph_index(word_glyph_index(40 + i)), screen_buffer_ptr, 0, 0)
                GoTo L_0040DB3E
            End If
            Call PAL_PutP(key_scan_map(i), key_action_map(i), word_glyph_index(word_glyph_index(40 + i)), screen_buffer_ptr, 4, 2)
L_0040DB3E:
        Next
        Call draw_battle_scene(1, 0)
    Wend
    retval = result
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub calc_player_attack_damage(ByRef targetRole, ByRef attackerRole, ByRef isCritical)
    If playerRoles(attackerRole, 4) Then
        Call player_attack_execute(targetRole, attackerRole)
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If player_status(targetRole, 8) > 0 Then
        baseDamage = 1
    Else
        baseDamage = 0
    End If
    j = 0
    For i = 0 To baseDamage
        atkPower = get_player_attribute_total(attackerRole, 17)
        defPower = calc_sprite_y_offset(isCritical, 4)
        k = (calc_battle_damage(atkPower, enemy_runtime_data(isCritical).defense + defPower) * 2) \ enemy_runtime_data(isCritical).physicalResistance
        Call role_physical_attack(targetRole, isCritical, k, baseDamage)
        j = j + k
    Next
    Call reset_battle_sprite_pos(targetRole)
    Call draw_battle_scene(1, 0)
    enemy_battle_data(isCritical).hp = enemy_battle_data(isCritical).hp - j
    If enemy_battle_data(isCritical).hp > 0 Then
        enemy_battle_data(isCritical).x = enemy_battle_data(isCritical).origX
        enemy_battle_data(isCritical).y = enemy_battle_data(isCritical).origY
    Else
        battle_enemy_hp = enemy_runtime_data(isCritical).deathSound
    End If
    rng = VB40032.rtcRandomNext()
    playerExp(attackerRole, 1).count = CSng (playerExp(attackerRole, 1).count) + (rng * CSng (2))
    playerExp(attackerRole, 3).count = playerExp(attackerRole, 3).count + 1
    Exit Sub
End Sub

Sub select_battle_action(ByRef menuType)
    menuItemSelected = 1
L_0040DE2A:
    If menuType = 0 Then
        menuStarted = 1
    Else
        menuStarted = 4
    End If
    result = select_item_with_filter(magic_select_tmp, -1, menuStarted)
    Call draw_battle_status_bar()
    If result >= 0 Then
        tmp = 0
        tmp5 = menuType
        If tmp5 = 0 Then
            Call PAL_GetBin(tmp2, objects(result).data6, 4)
            If tmp2 = 0 Then
                tmp3 = select_battle_target()
                If tmp3 < 0 Then
                    tmp = -2
                    GoTo L_0040E00C
                End If
                battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).actionType = 3
                battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).target = tmp3
                GoTo L_0040E00C
            End If
            tmp3 = -1
            battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).actionType = 3
            battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).target = tmp3
            GoTo L_0040E00C
        End If
        If tmp5 = 1 Then
            Call PAL_GetBin(tmp2, objects(result).data6, 4)
            If tmp2 = 0 Then
                tmp4 = select_enemy_target()
                If tmp4 < 0 Then
                    tmp = -2
                    GoTo L_0040E00C
                End If
                battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).actionType = 4
                battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).target = tmp4
                GoTo L_0040E00C
            End If
            tmp4 = battle_curr_role_idx
            battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).actionType = 4
            battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).target = tmp4
        End If
L_0040E00C:
        If tmp = -2 Then
            GoTo L_0040DE2A
        End If
        battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).itemID = result
        battle_role_action(battle_curr_role_idx).invIndex = magic_select_tmp
        inventory(magic_select_tmp).amountInUse = inventory(magic_select_tmp).amountInUse + 1
    Else
        menuItemSelected = -2
    End If
